Prompt:
As a Customer Support Representative, I want the Email Router system to automatically categorize incoming emails so that I can focus on handling complex customer inquiries rather than routine ones.

As an SME (Subject Matter Expert), I want the Email Router system to intelligently route complex communications to me based on content analysis so that I can efficiently address inquiries within my expertise.

As an IT Administrator, I want the Email Router system to provide a dashboard displaying performance metrics so that I can monitor system performance and make necessary adjustments for optimal operation.

As a Customer Support Representative, I want the Email Router system to generate draft responses for routine inquiries so that I can review and approve them before sending, ensuring consistent messaging.

As an SME (Subject Matter Expert), I want the Email Router system to include relevant context and metadata when routing emails to me so that I have all the necessary information to respond effectively.

As an IT Administrator, I want the Email Router system to handle a minimum of 10,000 emails per hour without performance degradation to ensure scalability as the organization grows.

As a Customer Support Representative, I want the Email Router system to process and classify incoming emails within 5 seconds of receipt so that I can respond promptly to customer inquiries.

As an SME (Subject Matter Expert), I want the Email Router system to only receive relevant, complex inquiries so that I can focus on addressing inquiries within my expertise, improving efficiency.

--- Interaction 1 ---
 Step 1: Worker agent generates a response to the prompt
Prompt:
1. Define the desired outcome for each action in the development of the Email Router system:

- Email Ingestion:
Desired Outcome: The system should connect to designated email services to retrieve incoming messages in real-time and preprocess emails to extract metadata such as sender, recipient, subject, and timestamp. This ensures that all incoming emails are captured and relevant information is extracted for further processing.

- Message Classification:
Desired Outcome: The system should analyze email content to determine intent and category using LLM-based models and assign a confidence score to each classification. This ensures that emails are accurately categorized and prioritized for appropriate handling.

- Knowledge Base Retrieval:
Desired Outcome: The system should search the knowledge base for relevant information corresponding to the classified intent and update the knowledge base with new information from resolved inquiries. This ensures that responses are based on up-to-date and accurate information.

- Response Generation:
Desired Outcome: The system should generate draft responses for routine inquiries using the RAG system and provide an interface for human review and approval of generated responses. This ensures that responses are generated efficiently and accurately before being sent out to customers.

- Email Routing:
Desired Outcome: The system should forward complex or high-confidence emails to the appropriate SME based on predefined rules and include relevant context and metadata when routing emails to SMEs. This ensures that emails are directed to the right person for timely and accurate responses.

- User Interface:
Desired Outcome: The system should provide a dashboard displaying performance metrics such as response times and accuracy, offer configuration options for managing the knowledge base, routing rules, and system settings, and allow manual intervention to override automated processes when necessary. This ensures that users can monitor system performance, make necessary adjustments, and intervene when needed for exceptional cases.

- Incoming messages are retrieved in real-time.

Dependencies: None

Task ID: 2

Prompt:
The product features of the Email Router system are as follows:

1. Email Ingestion System:
   - Seamless integration with email services via SMTP, IMAP, and RESTful APIs.
   - Real-time email retrieval and preprocessing to extract relevant metadata and content.

2. Message Classification Module:
   - Utilization of LLM-based classifiers to analyze email content and determine intent and category.
   - Assignment of confidence scores to decide between automated responses and manual handling.

3. Knowledge Base Integration:
   - Implementation of a vector database for efficient storage and retrieval of organizational knowledge.
   - Continuous learning mechanism to update the knowledge base with new information from resolved inquiries.

4. Response Generation Engine:
   - Deployment of a RAG system to generate contextually accurate and human-like responses.
   - Incorporation of an approval workflow for reviewing and editing automated responses before dispatch.

5. Routing Logic:
   - Development of a rules-based engine to assign emails to appropriate SMEs based on content analysis.
   - Context-aware forwarding that includes relevant metadata and previous correspondence history.

6. User Interface:
   - Creation of a comprehensive dashboard for monitoring system performance, including metrics on response times and accuracy.
   - Provision of a configuration panel for managing the knowledge base, routing rules, and system settings.
   - Implementation of manual override options to allow human intervention when necessary.

Description: This feature enables seamless integration with email services via SMTP, IMAP, and RESTful APIs. It allows for real-time email retrieval and preprocessing to extract relevant metadata and content.

Key Functionality: Integration with multiple email services, real-time email retrieval, metadata extraction.

User Benefit: Users benefit from a streamlined process of receiving and preprocessing emails in real-time, ensuring timely access to relevant information.

Feature Name: Message Classification Module

Description: The Message Classification Module utilizes LLM-based classifiers to analyze email content and determine intent and category. It assigns confidence scores to decide between automated responses and manual handling.

Key Functionality: Analysis of email content, intent determination, confidence scoring.

User Benefit: Users benefit from accurate categorization of emails, enabling efficient handling and appropriate responses based on the content.

Feature Name: Knowledge Base Integration

Description: This feature involves the implementation of a vector database for efficient storage and retrieval of organizational knowledge. It includes a continuous learning mechanism to update the knowledge base with new information from resolved inquiries.

Key Functionality: Efficient storage and retrieval of organizational knowledge, continuous learning mechanism.

User Benefit: Users benefit from easy access to relevant organizational knowledge and updated information, enhancing response accuracy and consistency.

Feature Name: Response Generation Engine

Description: The Response Generation Engine deploys a RAG system to generate contextually accurate and human-like responses. It includes an approval workflow for reviewing and editing automated responses before dispatch.

Key Functionality: Contextually accurate response generation, approval workflow.

User Benefit: Users benefit from the generation of accurate responses to inquiries, saving time and ensuring consistent messaging.

Feature Name: Routing Logic

Description: The Routing Logic feature involves the development of a rules-based engine to assign emails to appropriate SMEs based on content analysis. It includes context-aware forwarding that includes relevant metadata and previous correspondence history.

Key Functionality: Rules-based email assignment, context-aware forwarding.

User Benefit: Users benefit from efficient routing of emails to the right subject matter experts, ensuring timely and accurate responses to complex inquiries.

Feature Name: User Interface

Description: The User Interface feature includes the creation of a comprehensive dashboard for monitoring system performance, including metrics on response times and accuracy. It provides a configuration panel for managing the knowledge base, routing rules, and system settings, with manual override options for human intervention when necessary.

Key Functionality: Performance monitoring dashboard, configuration panel, manual override options.

User Benefit: Users benefit from easy monitoring of system performance, efficient management of settings, and the ability to intervene manually when needed, ensuring smooth operation of the system.

   - Implement a continuous learning mechanism to update the knowledge base with new information from resolved inquiries.

4. Response Generation Engine:
   - Deploy a RAG system to generate contextually accurate and human-like responses.
   - Incorporate an approval workflow for reviewing and editing automated responses before dispatch.

5. Routing Logic:
   - Develop a rules-based engine to assign emails to appropriate SMEs based on content analysis.
   - Implement context-aware forwarding that includes relevant metadata and previous correspondence history.

6. User Interface:
   - Create a comprehensive dashboard for monitoring system performance, including metrics on response times and accuracy.
   - Provide a configuration panel for managing the knowledge base, routing rules, and system settings.
   - Implement manual override options to allow human intervention when necessary.

These tasks can be allocated to developers with expertise in backend development, natural language processing, machine learning, user interface design, email systems, AI technologies, and database management. Assigning tasks based on developers' skills and availability will help ensure the successful and timely completion of the Email Router project.

Prompt:
Development Plan for Email Router System

1. Define User Stories:
- Identify user stories based on the functional requirements outlined in the Product Specification Document.
- Break down each requirement into specific tasks that need to be completed to implement the functionality.

2. Task Prioritization:
- Prioritize tasks based on their importance to achieving the primary objectives of the project.
- Ensure that tasks critical to reducing response time, increasing efficiency, improving routing accuracy, enhancing consistency, and liberating staff resources are given high priority.

3. Task Assignment:
- Assign tasks to development team members based on their expertise and availability.
- Ensure that each team member understands their responsibilities and deadlines for task completion.

4. Development Environment Setup:
- Set up the development environment with the necessary tools and resources for building the Email Router system.
- Ensure that version control systems are in place to track changes and facilitate collaboration among team members.

5. Email Ingestion System Development:
- Develop the email ingestion system to connect to designated email services and retrieve incoming messages in real-time.
- Implement preprocessing of emails to extract metadata such as sender, recipient, subject, and timestamp.

6. Message Classification Module Development:
- Develop the message classification module using LLM-based classifiers to analyze email content and determine intent and category.
- Implement confidence scoring to guide subsequent actions for automated responses or manual handling.

7. Knowledge Base Integration:
- Integrate a vector database for efficient storage and retrieval of organizational knowledge.
- Implement a continuous learning mechanism to update the knowledge base with new information from resolved inquiries.

8. Response Generation Engine Development:
- Develop the response generation engine using a RAG system to generate contextually accurate and human-like responses.
- Incorporate an approval workflow for reviewing and editing automated responses before dispatch.

9. Routing Logic Development:
- Develop a rules-based engine to assign emails to appropriate SMEs based on content analysis.
- Implement context-aware forwarding that includes relevant metadata and previous correspondence history.

10. User Interface Development:
- Develop a comprehensive dashboard for monitoring system performance, including metrics on response times and accuracy.
- Provide a configuration panel for managing the knowledge base, routing rules, and system settings.
- Implement manual override options to allow human intervention when necessary.

11. Testing and Quality Assurance:
- Conduct thorough testing of each module to ensure functionality, performance, and reliability.
- Perform user acceptance testing to validate that the system meets the specified requirements and objectives.

12. Deployment and Integration:
- Deploy the Email Router system within the organization's existing IT infrastructure.
- Integrate the system with standard email services and databases while ensuring data privacy and compliance with regulations.

13. Monitoring and Maintenance:
- Set up monitoring tools to track system performance, uptime, and email processing capabilities.
- Establish a maintenance plan to address any issues that arise post-deployment and ensure continuous improvement through feedback loops and model training.

By following this development plan, the Email Router system can be successfully built and deployed to address the organization's email management challenges and achieve the specified objectives.

Prompt:
**User Stories:**

1. As a customer support representative, I want to spend less time on routine inquiries so that I can focus on complex customer issues.
2. As an SME, I want to receive only relevant, complex inquiries to improve efficiency and job satisfaction.
3. As an IT administrator, I want a system that seamlessly integrates with existing email platforms and databases for easy configuration and monitoring.

**Features:**

1. Email Ingestion System:
   - Connect to designated email services to retrieve incoming messages in real-time.
   - Preprocess emails to extract metadata such as sender, recipient, subject, and timestamp.

2. Message Classification Module:
   - Analyze email content to determine intent and category using LLM-based models.
   - Assign a confidence score to each classification to guide subsequent actions.

3. Knowledge Base Integration:
   - Search the knowledge base for relevant information corresponding to the classified intent.
   - Update the knowledge base with new information from resolved inquiries.

4. Response Generation Engine:
   - Generate draft responses for routine inquiries using the RAG system.
   - Provide an interface for human review and approval of generated responses.

5. Routing Logic:
   - Forward complex or high-confidence emails to the appropriate SME based on predefined rules.
   - Include relevant context and metadata when routing emails to SMEs.

6. User Interface:
   - Provide a dashboard displaying performance metrics such as response times and accuracy.
   - Offer configuration options for managing the knowledge base, routing rules, and system settings.
   - Allow manual intervention to override automated processes when necessary.

**Development Tasks:**

1. Set up email ingestion system:
   - Establish connections to designated email services.
   - Implement real-time email retrieval and preprocessing.

2. Develop message classification module:
   - Integrate LLM-based classifiers for email content analysis.
   - Assign confidence scores to classifications.

3. Implement knowledge base integration:
   - Create a vector database for efficient storage and retrieval.
   - Set up continuous learning mechanism for knowledge base updates.

4. Deploy response generation engine:
   - Deploy RAG system for generating contextually accurate responses.
   - Incorporate approval workflow for reviewing and editing responses.

5. Develop routing logic:
   - Develop rules-based engine for assigning emails to SMEs.
   - Implement context-aware forwarding with relevant metadata.

6. Design user interface:
   - Create a dashboard for displaying performance metrics.
   - Develop configuration panel for managing system settings.
   - Implement manual override options for human intervention.

7. Ensure system performance:
   - Ensure email processing and classification within 5 seconds.
   - Maintain 99.9% uptime for continuous email processing capabilities.
   - Handle a minimum of 10,000 emails per hour without performance degradation.

8. Ensure system security:
   - Implement data encryption using AES-256 encryption.
   - Implement Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) and Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA).
   - Ensure compliance with GDPR, CCPA, and other data protection regulations.

This comprehensive development plan outlines the user stories, features, and tasks required for the successful implementation of the Email Router system.

   - Verify that personally identifiable information (PII) is appropriately handled and anonymized when necessary.

6. Implement Feedback Loop:
   - Integrate a feedback loop mechanism to capture ongoing user feedback and system performance data for continuous improvement.
   - Use feedback to iterate on system features, knowledge base updates, and routing rules to enhance overall system efficiency and effectiveness.

1. Schedule Regular Development Plan Reviews: Set up recurring meetings with the development team to review the current status of the development plan. This could be done weekly, bi-weekly, or monthly, depending on the project timeline and complexity.

2. Share the Development Plan: Distribute the development plan document outlining the tasks, timelines, and responsibilities to all team members before the review meeting. Ensure that everyone has access to the most up-to-date version of the plan.

3. Discuss Progress: During the review meetings, go through each task in the development plan and discuss the progress made, any challenges encountered, and any adjustments needed. Ensure that team members provide updates on their respective tasks.

5. Ensure Alignment with Objectives: Verify that the tasks in the development plan align with the project objectives and that progress is contributing towards achieving the defined goals.

4. Address Dependencies and Roadblocks: Identify any dependencies between tasks and address any roadblocks that may be hindering progress. Encourage open communication among team members to resolve issues collaboratively.

5. Ensure Alignment with Objectives: Verify that the tasks in the development plan align with the project objectives and that progress is contributing towards achieving the defined goals.

6. Update the Plan as Needed: Based on the discussions during the review meetings, update the development plan with any changes, new tasks, revised timelines, or resource allocations. Ensure that all team members are aware of any updates.

7. Document Meeting Minutes: Keep detailed notes of each review meeting, including action items, decisions made, and any follow-up tasks assigned to team members. Share these meeting minutes with the team for reference.

By following these steps and regularly reviewing the development plan with the team, you can ensure alignment, track progress, address issues promptly, and keep the project on course towards successful implementation of the Email Router system.

1. Monitoring System Performance: Develop a module that tracks key performance metrics such as response times, routing accuracy, and efficiency levels.

2. Feedback Collection: Implement a feature that collects feedback from users, including customer support representatives, SMEs, and IT administrators, to identify areas for improvement.

3. Data Analysis: Utilize analytics capabilities to analyze communication trends, identify bottlenecks, and assess progress towards project objectives.

4. Plan Adjustment: Based on the data analysis and feedback collected, update the project plan as needed to ensure alignment with primary and secondary objectives.

5. Continuous Improvement: Integrate tools for model training and knowledge base updates to enhance system performance over time.

Worker Agent Response:
Development Tasks for Implementing Feedback Loop Mechanism in Email Router System:

1. Define Requirements:
   - Identify key performance metrics to be tracked, such as response times, routing accuracy, and efficiency levels.
   - Specify the data points to be collected for feedback analysis from users.

2. Design Monitoring Module:
   - Develop a module that can capture and store performance metrics in real-time.
   - Design a dashboard interface to display the tracked metrics for easy monitoring.

3. Implement Feedback Collection Feature:
   - Integrate feedback collection forms within the user interface for customer support representatives, SMEs, and IT administrators.
   - Store feedback data in a centralized database for analysis.

4. Develop Data Analysis Capabilities:
   - Implement analytics tools to process and analyze the collected data.
   - Create algorithms to identify trends, bottlenecks, and progress towards project objectives.

5. Plan Adjustment Mechanism:
   - Establish a process to review data analysis results and feedback to determine necessary plan adjustments.
   - Define criteria for updating the project plan based on the identified areas for improvement.

6. Integrate Continuous Improvement Tools:
   - Develop mechanisms for model training to enhance the system's performance over time.
   - Implement processes for updating the knowledge base with new information from resolved inquiries.

Feature Name: Email Ingestion System

Description: This feature enables seamless integration with email services via SMTP, IMAP, and RESTful APIs. It allows for real-time email retrieval and preprocessing to extract relevant metadata and content.

Key Functionality: Integration with multiple email services, real-time email retrieval, metadata extraction.

User Benefit: Users benefit from a streamlined process of receiving and preprocessing emails in real-time, ensuring timely access to relevant information.

Feature Name: Message Classification Module

Description: The Message Classification Module utilizes LLM-based classifiers to analyze email content and determine intent and category. It assigns confidence scores to decide between automated responses and manual handling.

Key Functionality: Analysis of email content, intent determination, confidence scoring.

User Benefit: Users benefit from accurate categorization of emails, enabling efficient handling and appropriate responses based on the content.

Feature Name: Knowledge Base Integration

Description: This feature involves the implementation of a vector database for efficient storage and retrieval of organizational knowledge. It includes a continuous learning mechanism to update the knowledge base with new information from resolved inquiries.

Key Functionality: Efficient storage and retrieval of organizational knowledge, continuous learning mechanism.

User Benefit: Users benefit from easy access to relevant organizational knowledge and updated information, enhancing response accuracy and consistency.

Feature Name: Response Generation Engine

Description: The Response Generation Engine deploys a RAG system to generate contextually accurate and human-like responses. It includes an approval workflow for reviewing and editing automated responses before dispatch.

Key Functionality: Contextually accurate response generation, approval workflow.

User Benefit: Users benefit from the generation of accurate responses to inquiries, saving time and ensuring consistent messaging.

Feature Name: Routing Logic

Description: The Routing Logic feature involves the development of a rules-based engine to assign emails to appropriate SMEs based on content analysis. It includes context-aware forwarding that includes relevant metadata and previous correspondence history.

Key Functionality: Rules-based email assignment, context-aware forwarding.

User Benefit: Users benefit from efficient routing of emails to the right subject matter experts, ensuring timely and accurate responses to complex inquiries.

Feature Name: User Interface

Description: The User Interface feature includes the creation of a comprehensive dashboard for monitoring system performance, including metrics on response times and accuracy. It provides a configuration panel for managing the knowledge base, routing rules, and system settings, with manual override options for human intervention when necessary.

Key Functionality: Performance monitoring dashboard, configuration panel, manual override options.

User Benefit: Users benefit from easy monitoring of system performance, efficient management of settings, and the ability to intervene manually when needed, ensuring smooth operation of the system.

As an IT Administrator, I want the Message Classification Module to utilize LLM-based classifiers to analyze email content and determine intent and category, so that the system can accurately classify incoming emails for further processing.

1. **Email Ingestion System:**

     - Seamless integration with email services via SMTP, IMAP, and RESTful APIs.

     - Preprocess emails to extract metadata such as sender, recipient, subject, and timestamp.

   - Scope:
     - Utilization of LLM-based classifiers to analyze email content and determine intent and category.
     - Assignment of confidence scores to decide between automated responses and manual handling.
   - Functionality:
     - Analyze email content to determine intent and category using LLM-based models.
     - Assign a confidence score to each classification to guide subsequent actions.

   - Scope:
     - Implementation of a vector database for efficient storage and retrieval of organizational knowledge.
     - Continuous learning mechanism to update the knowledge base with new information from resolved inquiries.
   - Functionality:
     - Search the knowledge base for relevant information corresponding to the classified intent.
     - Update the knowledge base with new information from resolved inquiries.

4. **Response Generation Engine:**
   - Scope:
     - Deployment of a RAG system to generate contextually accurate and human-like responses.
     - Incorporation of an approval workflow for reviewing and editing automated responses before dispatch.
   - Functionality:
     - Generate draft responses for routine inquiries using the RAG system.
     - Provide an interface for human review and approval of generated responses.

5. **Routing Logic:**
   - Scope:
     - Development of a rules-based engine to assign emails to appropriate SMEs based on content analysis.
     - Context-aware forwarding that includes relevant metadata and previous correspondence history.
   - Functionality:
     - Forward complex or high-confidence emails to the appropriate SME based on predefined rules.
     - Include relevant context and metadata when routing emails to SMEs.

6. **User Interface:**
   - Scope:
     - Creation of a comprehensive dashboard for monitoring system performance, including metrics on response times and accuracy.

     - Implementation of manual override options to allow human intervention when necessary.

Based on the provided user stories and features, the following development tasks can be defined for the Email Router system:

1. **Set up email ingestion system:**
   - Establish connections to designated email services.
   - Implement real-time email retrieval and preprocessing.

2. **Develop message classification module:**
   - Integrate LLM-based classifiers for email content analysis.
   - Assign confidence scores to classifications.

3. **Implement knowledge base integration:**
   - Create a vector database for efficient storage and retrieval.
   - Set up continuous learning mechanism for knowledge base updates.

4. **Deploy response generation engine:**
   - Deploy RAG system for generating contextually accurate responses.
   - Incorporate approval workflow for reviewing and editing responses.

5. **Develop routing logic:**
   - Develop rules-based engine for assigning emails to SMEs.
   - Implement context-aware forwarding with relevant metadata.

6. **Design user interface:**
   - Create a dashboard for displaying performance metrics.
   - Develop configuration panel for managing system settings.
   - Implement manual override options for human intervention.

7. **Ensure system performance:**
   - Ensure email processing and classification within 5 seconds.
   - Maintain 99.9% uptime for continuous email processing capabilities.
   - Handle a minimum of 10,000 emails per hour without performance degradation.

8. **Ensure system security:**
   - Implement data encryption using AES-256 encryption.
   - Implement Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) and Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA).
   - Ensure compliance with GDPR, CCPA, and other data protection regulations.

These tasks cover the development aspects necessary to meet the user stories and features outlined for the Email Router system.

2. **Examples of Task Dependencies**:

- Task 2 (Implement Email Preprocessing) is dependent on Task 1 (Establish Email Connection) as the preprocessing module requires established email connections to extract metadata.
- Task 3 (Develop Message Classification) is dependent on Task 2 (Implement Email Preprocessing) as the classification process relies on preprocessed email content.
- Task 4 (Integrate Knowledge Base) is dependent on Task 3 (Develop Message Classification) as the knowledge base needs to be updated with new information from resolved inquiries classified by the system.

By following this structured approach and managing dependencies between tasks effectively, the development of the Email Router system can proceed smoothly and efficiently.

Task ID: 1.3.2
Task Title: Message Classification Module Development
Related User Story: As a development team, we need to utilize LLM-based classifiers to analyze email content and determine intent and category for efficient email processing.
Description: This task involves developing the message classification module for the Email Router system. The module will use LLM-based classifiers to analyze the content of incoming emails, determine their intent and category, and assign confidence scores for automated responses.
Acceptance Criteria:
- Successful implementation of LLM-based classifiers for email content analysis.
- Accurate determination of email intent and category.
- Assignment of confidence scores to guide subsequent actions.
Estimated Effort: 3 weeks
Dependencies: Completion of System Design and Architecture Documentation.

1. Task ID: 1
   - Task Title: Review Performance Metrics
   - Related User Story: Improve Response Time and Efficiency
   - Description: Analyze response times and accuracy metrics from the dashboard to identify areas for improvement. Evaluate system uptime to meet the 99.9% reliability requirement. Assess scalability during peak hours.
   - Acceptance Criteria: Response time reduced by 60%, system uptime meets 99.9% requirement, system handles peak email volumes effectively.
   - Estimated Effort: 2 weeks
   - Dependencies: Access to performance metrics data, collaboration with IT team for scalability assessment.

2. Task ID: 2
   - Task Title: Gather User Feedback
   - Related User Story: Enhance User Experience
   - Description: Collect feedback from Customer Support Representatives, SMEs, and IT Administrators. Identify pain points and areas for enhancement based on user input.
   - Acceptance Criteria: Actionable feedback collected from all user classes, pain points identified and documented.
   - Estimated Effort: 1 week
   - Dependencies: Coordination with user representatives for feedback collection.

3. Task ID: 3
   - Task Title: Conduct Knowledge Base Analysis
   - Related User Story: Improve Response Accuracy
   - Description: Review knowledge base utilization and effectiveness in generating responses. Identify gaps requiring updates based on resolved inquiries.
   - Acceptance Criteria: Knowledge base updated with new information, gaps identified and documented.
   - Estimated Effort: 1.5 weeks
   - Dependencies: Access to knowledge base data, collaboration with subject matter experts.

4. Task ID: 4
   - Task Title: Evaluate Routing Accuracy
   - Related User Story: Enhance Email Routing
   - Description: Review routing logic to ensure correct assignment to SMEs. Assess accuracy of routing decisions based on content analysis and confidence scores.
   - Acceptance Criteria: Routing accuracy improved, routing decisions aligned with content analysis.
   - Estimated Effort: 1 week
   - Dependencies: Access to routing logic data, collaboration with routing team.

5. Task ID: 5
   - Task Title: Perform Security Audit
   - Related User Story: Ensure Data Protection
   - Description: Conduct security audit to ensure compliance with encryption standards, access control, and data privacy regulations. Verify appropriate handling of PII.
   - Acceptance Criteria: Security audit passed, compliance with encryption and data privacy regulations confirmed.
   - Estimated Effort: 2 weeks
   - Dependencies: Collaboration with security and compliance teams.

6. Task ID: 6
   - Task Title: Implement Feedback Loop
   - Related User Story: Continuous Improvement
   - Description: Integrate feedback loop mechanism to capture user feedback and system performance data. Use feedback to iterate on system features, knowledge base updates, and routing rules.
   - Acceptance Criteria: Feedback loop integrated, system improvements based on feedback implemented.
   - Estimated Effort: 1.5 weeks
   - Dependencies: Access to feedback loop tools, collaboration with user feedback team.

By following these defined tasks for review and iteration, the Email Router system can be continuously improved to meet the organization's objectives and user requirements effectively.
